# Smoldyn configuration for Lorenz '63 Task

dim 3
boundaries x -25 25
boundaries y -25 25
boundaries z -25 25

species Receptor Messenger ReceptorActive

difc Receptor 0
difc ReceptorActive 0
difc Messenger DIFFMESSENGER

color Receptor(all) maroon
color Messenger(all) orange
color ReceptorActive(up) fuchsia

display_size all(all) 3

time_start START_TIME
time_stop STOP_TIME
time_step TIME_STEP

start_surface receiver
  action both all reflect
  panel sphere TXRXDISTANCE 0 0 3 30 30
end_surface

reaction binding Messenger(fsoln) + Receptor(up) -> ReceptorActive(up) KON
reaction unbinding ReceptorActive(up) -> Messenger(fsoln) + Receptor(up) KOFF

surface_mol 500 Receptor(up) receiver all all

output_files allmolecules.txt
cmd b overwrite allmolecules.txt
cmd b molcountheader allmolecules.txt
cmd i START_TIME STOP_TIME SAMPLING_PERIOD molcount allmolecules.txt
